{"etymology_number":"1","lang_code":"en","pos":"adj","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"I bought my new ride from some nickel and dime used-car salesman.","type":"example"},{"text":"Don't waste your time with that; their operations are nickel and dime.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Small time; operating on a small scale; involving small amounts of money; petty or cheap."],"synonyms":[{"word":"two-bit"},{"word":"ten-cent"}],"tags":["US","colloquial","idiomatic"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˌnɪkəl ən ˈdaɪm/","tags":["US"]}],"upstream_line":362833,"upstream_offset":1749938604,"upstream_record_sha256":"bfb057257806e244fb863927697abeddbeddf3c07b9eac44ac2053496e71f952","word":"nickel and dime"}
{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"nickel and dimes","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"nickels and dimes","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"nickel and diming","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"nickeling and diming","tags":["US","participle","present"]},{"form":"nickelling and diming","tags":["UK","participle","present"]},{"form":"nickel and dimed","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"nickel and dimed","tags":["past"]},{"form":"nickeled and dimed","tags":["US","participle","past"]},{"form":"nickeled and dimed","tags":["US","past"]},{"form":"nickelled and dimed","tags":["UK","participle","past"]},{"form":"nickelled and dimed","tags":["UK","past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"I got nickel and dimed to death by the phone company's sneaky extra charges.","type":"example"},{"text":"It seems like a great offer, but they will just nickel and dime you until you've spent more than retail anyway.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To charge, or be charged, several unexpected small amounts of money, often in the form of fees, taxes, or related expenses to a venture, which when taken as a whole add up to a significant unexpected cost."],"tags":["US","colloquial","idiomatic"]},{"glosses":["To wear down in small increments; to quibble or obsess endlessly with (someone) over trifles."],"tags":["US","colloquial","figuratively","idiomatic"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˌnɪkəl ən ˈdaɪm/","tags":["US"]}],"upstream_line":362834,"upstream_offset":1749940804,"upstream_record_sha256":"5a8fefc9210e4cd9aa1bee80b777050c0aadcb46b547db09b04750ccdb7219e9","word":"nickel and dime"}
